Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the data_structures framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 749. The resulting operator achieves bound O(325/n^3).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the data_structures space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 749 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 325. QED.
